# Fair Market Rents: Connecticut

- Postal code: CT
- State FIPS: 09
- Counties: 0
- Towns: 169

HUD's Fair Market Rents for Connecticut, fiscal years 1983 to 2027: 169 towns, each with its rents by bedroom count and the FMR areas it has sat in. 106 of them have changed FMR area at least once (not counting 2006, when HUD renumbered every area).
